    1: package com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.application.generation;
    2: 
    3: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.model.ScientificFeatureVector;
    4: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.port.in.generation.ScientificFeatureGenerator;
    5: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.port.in.generation.model.ScientificFeatureGenerationRequest;
    6: 
    7: import java.util.Objects;
    8: 
    9: /**
   10:  * Application service responsible for coordinating scientific feature
   11:  * generation.
   12:  *
   13:  * <p>This service deliberately contains no instrument-specific extraction,
   14:  * persistence, transaction, framework, or transport logic. Its current
   15:  * responsibility is to validate the application input and delegate feature
   16:  * generation to the configured {@link ScientificFeatureGenerator} port.</p>
   17:  */
   18: public final class ScientificFeatureGenerationService {
   19: 
   20:     private final ScientificFeatureGenerator generator;
   21: 
   22:     /**
   23:      * Creates the scientific feature generation application service.
   24:      *
   25:      * @param generator scientific feature generator port
   26:      * @throws NullPointerException when {@code generator} is {@code null}
   27:      */
   28:     public ScientificFeatureGenerationService(
   29:             ScientificFeatureGenerator generator
   30:     ) {
   31:         this.generator = Objects.requireNonNull(
   32:                 generator,
   33:                 "generator must not be null"
   34:         );
   35:     }
   36: 
   37:     /**
   38:      * Generates a scientific feature vector from a validated generation
   39:      * request.
   40:      *
   41:      * @param request scientific feature generation request
   42:      * @return the vector returned by the configured generator
   43:      * @throws NullPointerException when {@code request} is {@code null}
   44:      */
   45:     public ScientificFeatureVector generate(
   46:             ScientificFeatureGenerationRequest request
   47:     ) {
   48:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   49:                 request,
   50:                 "request must not be null"
   51:         );
   52: 
   53:         return generator.generate(request);
   54:     }
   55: }

FILE: C:\TEAC2026\ilp_backend\adaptive-education-service\src\main\java\com\inclusive\adaptiveeducationservice\featurestore\scientific\domain\model\ScientificFeatureItem.java
    1: package com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.model;
    2: 
    3: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.valueobject.FeatureCode;
    4: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.valueobject.FeatureValue;
    5: 
    6: import java.util.Objects;
    7: 
    8: public record ScientificFeatureItem(
    9:         String id,
   10:         FeatureCode featureCode,
   11:         FeatureValue value,
   12:         String sourceAssessmentCode,
   13:         String sourceAdministrationId
   14: ) {
   15: 
   16:     private static final int MAXIMUM_ID_LENGTH = 120;
   17:     private static final int MAXIMUM_SOURCE_LENGTH = 100;
   18: 
   19:     public ScientificFeatureItem {
   20:         id = requireText(
   21:                 id,
   22:                 "id",
   23:                 MAXIMUM_ID_LENGTH
   24:         );
   25: 
   26:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   27:                 featureCode,
   28:                 "featureCode is required"
   29:         );
   30: 
   31:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   32:                 value,
   33:                 "featureValue is required"
   34:         );
   35: 
   36:         sourceAssessmentCode =
   37:                 normalizeOptional(
   38:                         sourceAssessmentCode,
   39:                         "sourceAssessmentCode",
   40:                         MAXIMUM_SOURCE_LENGTH
   41:                 );
   42: 
   43:         sourceAdministrationId =
   44:                 normalizeOptional(
   45:                         sourceAdministrationId,
   46:                         "sourceAdministrationId",
   47:                         MAXIMUM_SOURCE_LENGTH
   48:                 );
   49:     }
   50: 
   51:     private static String requireText(
   52:             String value,
   53:             String field,
   54:             int maximumLength
   55:     ) {
   56:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   57:                 value,
   58:                 field + " is required"
   59:         );
   60: 
   61:         String normalized = value.trim();
   62: 
   63:         if (normalized.isEmpty()) {
   64:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
   65:                     field + " must not be blank"
   66:             );
   67:         }
   68: 
   69:         if (normalized.length() > maximumLength) {
   70:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
   71:                     field
   72:                             + " must not exceed "
   73:                             + maximumLength
   74:                             + " characters"
   75:             );
   76:         }
   77: 
   78:         return normalized;
   79:     }
   80: 
   81:     private static String normalizeOptional(
   82:             String value,
   83:             String field,
   84:             int maximumLength
   85:     ) {
   86:         if (value == null) {
   87:             return null;
   88:         }
   89: 
   90:         String normalized = value.trim();
   91: 
   92:         if (normalized.isEmpty()) {
   93:             return null;
   94:         }
   95: 
   96:         if (normalized.length() > maximumLength) {
   97:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
   98:                     field
   99:                             + " must not exceed "
  100:                             + maximumLength
  101:                             + " characters"
  102:             );
  103:         }
  104: 
  105:         return normalized;
  106:     }
  107: }

    1: package com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.model;

   11: import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
   12: import java.util.List;
   13: import java.util.Map;
   14: import java.util.Objects;
   15: import java.util.Optional;
   16: 
   17: public final class ScientificFeatureVector {
   18: 
   19:     private final ScientificFeatureVectorId id;
   20:     private final ParticipantId participantId;
   21:     private final FeatureSetVersion featureSetVersion;
   22:     private final GeneratorVersion generatorVersion;
   23:     private final Instant featureCutoffAt;
   24:     private final Instant generatedAt;
   25:     private final int sourceObservationCount;
   26:     private final ScientificChecksum checksum;
   27:     private final List<ScientificFeatureItem> items;
   28:     private final Map<FeatureCode, ScientificFeatureItem> itemsByCode;
   29: 
   30:     public ScientificFeatureVector(
   31:             ScientificFeatureVectorId id,
   32:             ParticipantId participantId,
   33:             FeatureSetVersion featureSetVersion,
   34:             GeneratorVersion generatorVersion,
   35:             Instant featureCutoffAt,
   36:             Instant generatedAt,
   37:             int sourceObservationCount,
   38:             ScientificChecksum checksum,
   39:             List<ScientificFeatureItem> items
   40:     ) {
   41:         this.id =
   42:                 Objects.requireNonNull(
   43:                         id,
   44:                         "id is required"
   45:                 );
   46: 
   47:         this.participantId =
   48:                 Objects.requireNonNull(
   49:                         participantId,
   50:                         "participantId is required"
   51:                 );
   52: 
   53:         this.featureSetVersion =
   54:                 Objects.requireNonNull(
   55:                         featureSetVersion,
   56:                         "featureSetVersion is required"
   57:                 );
   58: 
   59:         this.generatorVersion =
   60:                 Objects.requireNonNull(
   61:                         generatorVersion,
   62:                         "generatorVersion is required"
   63:                 );
   64: 
   65:         this.featureCutoffAt =
   66:                 Objects.requireNonNull(
   67:                         featureCutoffAt,
   68:                         "featureCutoffAt is required"
   69:                 );
   70: 
   71:         this.generatedAt =
   72:                 Objects.requireNonNull(
   73:                         generatedAt,
   74:                         "generatedAt is required"
   75:                 );
   76: 
   77:         if (generatedAt.isBefore(featureCutoffAt)) {
   78:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
   79:                     "generatedAt must not be before featureCutoffAt"
   80:             );
   81:         }
   82: 
   83:         if (sourceObservationCount < 0) {
   84:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
   85:                     "sourceObservationCount must be non-negative"
   86:             );
   87:         }
   88: 
   89:         this.sourceObservationCount =
   90:                 sourceObservationCount;
   91: 
   92:         this.checksum =
   93:                 Objects.requireNonNull(
   94:                         checksum,
   95:                         "checksum is required"
   96:                 );
   97: 
   98:         this.itemsByCode =
   99:                 validateAndIndexItems(items);
  100: 
  101:         this.items =
  102:                 List.copyOf(
  103:                         itemsByCode.values()
  104:                 );
  105:     }
  106: 
  107:     public ScientificFeatureVectorId id() {
  108:         return id;
  109:     }
  110: 
  111:     public ParticipantId participantId() {
  112:         return participantId;

  114: 
  115:     public FeatureSetVersion featureSetVersion() {

  118:

  122:

  124:         return featureCutoffAt;
  125:     }
  126: 
  127:     public Instant generatedAt() {
  128:         return generatedAt;
  129:     }
  130: 
  131:     public int sourceObservationCount() {
  132:         return sourceObservationCount;
  133:     }
  134: 
  135:     public ScientificChecksum checksum() {
  136:         return checksum;
  137:     }
  138: 
  139:     public List<ScientificFeatureItem> items() {
  140:         return items;
  141:     }
  142: 
  143:     public int featureCount() {
  144:         return items.size();
  145:     }
  146: 
  147:     public boolean containsFeature(
  148:             FeatureCode featureCode
  149:     ) {
  150:         Objects.requireNonNull(
  151:                 featureCode,
  152:                 "featureCode is required"
  153:         );
  154: 
  155:         return itemsByCode.containsKey(
  156:                 featureCode
  157:         );
  158:     }
  159: 
  160:     public Optional<ScientificFeatureItem> findFeature(
  161:             FeatureCode featureCode
  162:     ) {
  163:         Objects.requireNonNull(
  164:                 featureCode,
  165:                 "featureCode is required"
  166:         );
  167: 
  168:         return Optional.ofNullable(
  169:                 itemsByCode.get(featureCode)
  170:         );
  171:     }
  172: 
  173:     public ScientificFeatureItem requireFeature(
  174:             FeatureCode featureCode
  175:     ) {
  176:         return findFeature(featureCode)
  177:                 .orElseThrow(() ->
  178:                         new IllegalArgumentException(
  179:                                 "Feature not found: "
  180:                                         + featureCode.value()
  181:                         )
  182:                 );
  183:     }
  184: 
  185:     private static Map<
  186:             FeatureCode,
  187:             ScientificFeatureItem
  188:             > validateAndIndexItems(
  189:             List<ScientificFeatureItem> items
  190:     ) {
  191:         Objects.requireNonNull(
  192:                 items,
  193:                 "items are required"
  194:         );
  195: 
  196:         if (items.isEmpty()) {
  197:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
  198:                     "items must not be empty"
  199:             );
  200:         }
  201: 
  202:         Map<
  203:                 FeatureCode,
  204:                 ScientificFeatureItem
  205:                 > indexed =
  206:                 new LinkedHashMap<>();
  207: 
  208:         for (ScientificFeatureItem item : items) {
  209:             Objects.requireNonNull(
  210:                     item,
  211:                     "feature item is required"
  212:             );
  213: 
  214:             ScientificFeatureItem previous =
  215:                     indexed.putIfAbsent(
  216:                             item.featureCode(),
  217:                             item
  218:                     );
  219: 
  220:             if (previous != null) {
  221:                 throw new IllegalArgumentException(
  222:                         "Duplicate feature code: "
  223:                                 + item.featureCode().value()
  224:                 );
  225:             }
  226:         }
  227: 
  228:         return Map.copyOf(indexed);
  229:     }
  230: }

    2: 
    3: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.model.ScientificFeatureItem;
    4: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.valueobject.FeatureSetVersion;
    5: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.valueobject.GeneratorVersion;
    6: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.valueobject.ParticipantId;
    7: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.valueobject.ScientificChecksum;
    8: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.valueobject.ScientificFeatureVectorId;
    9: 
   10: import java.time.Instant;
   11: import java.util.List;
   12: import java.util.Objects;
   13: 
   14: public record ScientificFeatureGenerationRequest(
   15:         ScientificFeatureVectorId vectorId,
   16:         ParticipantId participantId,
   17:         FeatureSetVersion featureSetVersion,
   18:         GeneratorVersion generatorVersion,
   19:         Instant featureCutoffAt,
   20:         Instant generatedAt,
   21:         int inputObservationCount,
   22:         ScientificChecksum checksum,
   23:         List<ScientificFeatureItem> extractedFeatures
   24: ) {
   25: 
   26:     public ScientificFeatureGenerationRequest {
   27:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   28:                 vectorId,
   29:                 "vectorId is required"
   30:         );
   31: 
   32:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   33:                 participantId,
   34:                 "participantId is required"
   35:         );
   36: 
   37:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   38:                 featureSetVersion,
   39:                 "featureSetVersion is required"
   40:         );
   41: 
   42:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   43:                 generatorVersion,
   44:                 "generatorVersion is required"
   45:         );
   46: 
   47:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   48:                 featureCutoffAt,
   49:                 "featureCutoffAt is required"
   50:         );
   51: 
   52:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   53:                 generatedAt,
   54:                 "generatedAt is required"
   55:         );
   56: 
   57:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   58:                 checksum,
   59:                 "checksum is required"
   60:         );
   61: 
   62:         if (generatedAt.isBefore(featureCutoffAt)) {
   63:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
   64:                     "generatedAt must not be before featureCutoffAt"
   65:             );
   66:         }
   67: 
   68:         if (inputObservationCount < 0) {
   69:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
   70:                     "inputObservationCount must be non-negative"
   71:             );
   72:         }
   73: 
   74:         if (extractedFeatures == null
   75:                 || extractedFeatures.isEmpty()) {
   76:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
   77:                     "At least one extracted feature is required"
   78:             );
   79:         }
   80: 
   81:         if (extractedFeatures.stream().anyMatch(Objects::isNull)) {
   82:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
   83:                     "extractedFeatures must not contain null elements"
   84:             );
   85:         }
   86: 
   87:         extractedFeatures =
   88:                 List.copyOf(extractedFeatures);
   89:     }
   90: 
   91:     public int featureCount() {
   92:         return extractedFeatures.size();
   93:     }
   94: }

FILE: C:\TEAC2026\ilp_backend\adaptive-education-service\src\main\java\com\inclusive\adaptiveeducationservice\featurestore\scientific\port\out\provider\ScientificFeatureProvider.java
    1: package com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.port.out.provider;
    2: 
    3: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.model.ScientificFeatureItem;
    4: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.port.in.generation.model.ScientificFeatureGenerationRequest;
    5: 
    6: import java.util.List;
    7: 
    8: /**
    9:  * Output port that supplies scientific feature items for a feature generation
   10:  * request.
   11:  *
   12:  * <p>Implementations may obtain or calculate features from a specific
   13:  * scientific, psychopedagogical, academic, or research source. This port
   14:  * remains independent from persistence, transport, framework, and
   15:  * infrastructure concerns.</p>
   16:  *
   17:  * <p>The generated feature vector remains the responsibility of the
   18:  * scientific feature generator. A provider supplies only the feature items
   19:  * required by that generation process.</p>
   20:  */
   21: @FunctionalInterface
   22: public interface ScientificFeatureProvider {
   23: 
   24:     /**
   25:      * Provides scientific feature items associated with the supplied
   26:      * generation request.
   27:      *
   28:      * @param request scientific feature generation request
   29:      * @return scientific feature items supplied by this provider
   30:      */
   31:     List<ScientificFeatureItem> provide(
   32:             ScientificFeatureGenerationRequest request
   33:     );
   34: }

FILE: C:\TEAC2026\ilp_backend\adaptive-education-service\src\main\java\com\inclusive\adaptiveeducationservice\featurestore\scientific\provider\kolb\KolbScientificFeatureProvider.java
    1: package com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.provider.kolb;
    2: 
    3: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.assessment.entity.KolbAssessmentResultEntity;
    4: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.assessment.repository.KolbAssessmentResultRepository;
    5: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.model.ScientificFeatureItem;
    6: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.valueobject.FeatureCode;
    7: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.domain.valueobject.FeatureValue;
    8: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.port.in.generation.model.ScientificFeatureGenerationRequest;
    9: import com.inclusive.adaptiveeducationservice.featurestore.scientific.port.out.provider.ScientificFeatureProvider;
   10: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   11: 
   12: import java.util.List;
   13: import java.util.Objects;
   14: 
   15: @Component
   16: public final class KolbScientificFeatureProvider
   17:         implements ScientificFeatureProvider {
   18: 
   19:     static final String SOURCE_ASSESSMENT_CODE = "KOLB";
   20: 
   21:     static final String CE_SCORE_CODE = "KOLB_CE_SCORE";
   22:     static final String RO_SCORE_CODE = "KOLB_RO_SCORE";
   23:     static final String AC_SCORE_CODE = "KOLB_AC_SCORE";
   24:     static final String AE_SCORE_CODE = "KOLB_AE_SCORE";
   25:     static final String AC_MINUS_CE_CODE = "KOLB_AC_MINUS_CE";
   26:     static final String AE_MINUS_RO_CODE = "KOLB_AE_MINUS_RO";
   27:     static final String LEARNING_STYLE_CODE = "KOLB_LEARNING_STYLE";
   28: 
   29:     private final KolbAssessmentResultRepository resultRepository;
   30: 
   31:     public KolbScientificFeatureProvider(
   32:             KolbAssessmentResultRepository resultRepository
   33:     ) {
   34:         this.resultRepository = Objects.requireNonNull(
   35:                 resultRepository,
   36:                 "resultRepository is required"
   37:         );
   38:     }
   39: 
   40:     @Override
   41:     public List<ScientificFeatureItem> provide(
   42:             ScientificFeatureGenerationRequest request
   43:     ) {
   44:         Objects.requireNonNull(
   45:                 request,
   46:                 "request is required"
   47:         );
   48: 
   49:         return resultRepository
   50:                 .findFirstByStudentIdAndCreatedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCreatedAtDesc(
   51:                         request.participantId().value(),
   52:                         request.featureCutoffAt()
   53:                 )
   54:                 .map(this::toScientificFeatures)
   55:                 .orElseGet(List::of);
   56:     }
   57: 
   58:     private List<ScientificFeatureItem> toScientificFeatures(
   59:             KolbAssessmentResultEntity result
   60:     ) {
   61:         double abstractConcreteAxis =
   62:                 result.getScoreAC() - result.getScoreCE();
   63: 
   64:         double activeReflectiveAxis =
   65:                 result.getScoreAE() - result.getScoreRO();
   66: 
   67:         return List.of(
   68:                 numericFeature(
   69:                         result,
   70:                         CE_SCORE_CODE,
   71:                         result.getScoreCE()
   72:                 ),
   73:                 numericFeature(
   74:                         result,
   75:                         RO_SCORE_CODE,
   76:                         result.getScoreRO()
   77:                 ),
   78:                 numericFeature(
   79:                         result,
   80:                         AC_SCORE_CODE,
   81:                         result.getScoreAC()
   82:                 ),
   83:                 numericFeature(
   84:                         result,
   85:                         AE_SCORE_CODE,
   86:                         result.getScoreAE()
   87:                 ),
   88:                 numericFeature(
   89:                         result,
   90:                         AC_MINUS_CE_CODE,
   91:                         abstractConcreteAxis
   92:                 ),
   93:                 numericFeature(
   94:                         result,
   95:                         AE_MINUS_RO_CODE,
   96:                         activeReflectiveAxis
   97:                 ),
   98:                 textFeature(
   99:                         result,
  100:                         LEARNING_STYLE_CODE,
  101:                         result.getLearningStyle()
  102:                 )
  103:         );
  104:     }
  105: 
  106:     private ScientificFeatureItem numericFeature(
  107:             KolbAssessmentResultEntity result,
  108:             String featureCode,
  109:             double value
  110:     ) {
  111:         return feature(
  112:                 result,
  113:                 featureCode,
  114:                 FeatureValue.numeric(value)
  115:         );
  116:     }
  117: 
  118:     private ScientificFeatureItem textFeature(
  119:             KolbAssessmentResultEntity result,
  120:             String featureCode,
  121:             String value
  122:     ) {
  123:         return feature(
  124:                 result,
  125:                 featureCode,
  126:                 FeatureValue.text(value)
  127:         );
  128:     }
  129: 
  130:     private ScientificFeatureItem feature(
  131:             KolbAssessmentResultEntity result,
  132:             String featureCode,
  133:             FeatureValue value
  134:     ) {
  135:         return new ScientificFeatureItem(
  136:                 featureId(result.getId(), featureCode),
  137:                 new FeatureCode(featureCode),
  138:                 value,
  139:                 SOURCE_ASSESSMENT_CODE,
  140:                 result.getId()
  141:         );
  142:     }
  143: 
  144:     private String featureId(
  145:             String administrationId,
  146:             String featureCode
  147:     ) {
  148:         return administrationId + "-" + featureCode;
  149:     }
  150: }

   20: /**
   21:  * Unit tests for {@link ScientificFeatureGenerationService}.
   22:  *
   23:  * <p>The test suite verifies constructor validation, input validation,
   24:  * delegation semantics, result identity, and exception propagation without
   25:  * starting Spring or accessing persistence infrastructure.</p>
   26:  */
   27: @ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
   28: class ScientificFeatureGenerationServiceTest {
   29: 
   30:     @Mock
   31:     private ScientificFeatureGenerator generator;
   32: 
   33:     @Mock
   34:     private ScientificFeatureGenerationRequest request;
   35: 
   36:     @Mock
   37:     private ScientificFeatureVector generatedVector;
   38: 
   39:     private ScientificFeatureGenerationService service;
   40: 
   41:     @BeforeEach
   42:     void setUp() {
   43:         service =
   44:                 new ScientificFeatureGenerationService(
   45:                         generator
   46:                 );
   47:     }
   48: 
   49:     @Test
   50:     void shouldRejectNullGenerator() {
   51:         assertThatNullPointerException()
   52:                 .isThrownBy(() ->
   53:                         new ScientificFeatureGenerationService(
   54:                                 null
   55:                         )
   56:                 )
   57:                 .withMessage(
   58:                         "generator must not be null"
   59:                 );
   60:     }
   61: 
   62:     @Test
   63:     void shouldRejectNullRequestWithoutCallingGenerator() {
   64:         assertThatNullPointerException()
   65:                 .isThrownBy(() ->
   66:                         service.generate(null)
   67:                 )
   68:                 .withMessage(
   69:                         "request must not be null"
   70:                 );
   71: 
   72:         verifyNoInteractions(generator);
   73:     }
   74: 
   75:     @Test
   76:     void shouldDelegateGenerationAndReturnSameVector() {
   77:         when(generator.generate(request))
   78:                 .thenReturn(generatedVector);
   79: 
   80:         ScientificFeatureVector result =
   81:                 service.generate(request);
   82: 
   83:         assertThat(result)
   84:                 .isSameAs(generatedVector);
   85: 
   86:         verify(generator)
   87:                 .generate(request);
   88: 
   89:         verifyNoMoreInteractions(generator);
   90:     }
   91: 
   92:     @Test
   93:     void shouldPropagateGeneratorFailureWithoutWrappingIt() {
   94:         IllegalStateException generationFailure =
   95:                 new IllegalStateException(
   96:                         "Scientific generation failed"
   97:                 );
   98: 
   99:         when(generator.generate(request))
  100:                 .thenThrow(generationFailure);
  101: 
  102:         assertThatThrownBy(() ->
  103:                 service.generate(request)
  104:         )
  105:                 .isSameAs(generationFailure);
  106: 
  107:         verify(generator)
  108:                 .generate(request);
  109: 
  110:         verifyNoMoreInteractions(generator);
  111:     }
  112: }

   16: class ScientificFeatureGenerationRunTest {

   19:             Instant.parse(
   20:                     "2026-07-27T10:00:00Z"
   21:             );
   22: 
   23:     private static final Instant STARTED_AT =
   24:             Instant.parse(
   25:                     "2026-07-27T10:00:01Z"
   26:             );
   27: 
   28:     @Test
   29:     void shouldStartGenerationRun() {

   32:

   34:                 .isEqualTo("SFGR-001");
   35:

   37:                 .isEqualTo(
   38:                         ScientificFeatureGenerationStatus.STARTED
   39:                 );

   44:         assertThat(run.isCompleted())
   45:                 .isFalse();
   46: 
   47:         assertThat(run.isFailed())
   48:                 .isFalse();
   49:

   51:                 .isNull();
   52:

   54:                 .isNull();
   55: 
   56:         assertThat(run.errorMessage())
   57:                 .isNull();
   58: 
   59:         assertThat(run.duration())
   60:                 .isEmpty();
   61:     }
   62: 
   63:     @Test
   64:     void shouldCompleteStartedRun() {
   65:         Instant completedAt =
   66:                 STARTED_AT.plusSeconds(5);
   67: 
   68:         ScientificFeatureGenerationRun completed =
   69:                 startedRun().complete(
   70:                         new ScientificFeatureVectorId(
   71:                                 "SFV-001"
   72:                         ),
   73:                         completedAt
   74:                 );
   75: 
   76:         assertThat(completed.isCompleted())
   77:                 .isTrue();
   78: 
   79:         assertThat(completed.status())
   80:                 .isEqualTo(
   81:                         ScientificFeatureGenerationStatus.COMPLETED
   82:                 );
   83: 
   84:         assertThat(
   85:                 completed.featureVectorId().value()
   86:         ).isEqualTo("SFV-001");
   87: 
   88:         assertThat(completed.completedAt())
   89:                 .isEqualTo(completedAt);
   90: 
   91:         assertThat(completed.errorMessage())
   92:                 .isNull();
   93: 
   94:         assertThat(completed.duration())
   95:                 .contains(
   96:                         Duration.ofSeconds(5)
   97:                 );
   98:     }
   99: 
  100:     @Test
  101:     void shouldFailStartedRun() {
  102:         Instant completedAt =
  103:                 STARTED_AT.plusSeconds(3);
  104:

  109:                 );

   81:

   86:     }

  100:                 vectorEntity();

  103:                 new ScientificFeatureGenerationRunMapper()

  106:                                 vector
  107:                         );
  108: 
  109:         when(vectorRepository.findById("SFV-001"))
  110:                 .thenReturn(Optional.of(vector));
  111: 
  112:         when(runRepository.save(any()))
  113:                 .thenReturn(entity);
  114: 
  115:         ScientificFeatureGenerationRun result =
  116:                 adapter.save(domain);
  117: 
  118:         assertThat(result.isCompleted())
  119:                 .isTrue();
  120: 
  121:         assertThat(result.featureVectorId())
  122:                 .isEqualTo(
  123:                         new ScientificFeatureVectorId(
  124:                                 "SFV-001"
  125:                         )
  126:                 );
  127: 
  128:         verify(vectorRepository)
  129:                 .findById("SFV-001");
  130:     }
  131:

  475:     private ScientificFeatureGenerationRun startedDomainRun() {

  481:                 new FeatureSetVersion(
  482:                         "ILP_SCIENTIFIC_BASELINE_V1"
  483:                 ),
  484:                 new GeneratorVersion(
  485:                         "SCIENTIFIC_FEATURE_GENERATOR_V1"
  486:                 ),
  487:                 CUTOFF,
  488:                 STARTED_AT,
  489:                 2
  490:         );
  491:     }

  493:     private ScientificFeatureGenerationRunEntity startedEntity() {

  495:                 "SFGR-001",

  498:                 "SCIENTIFIC_FEATURE_GENERATOR_V1",
  499:                 CUTOFF,
  500:                 STARTED_AT,
  501:                 2
  502:         );
  503:     }
  504: 
  505:     private ScientificFeatureVectorEntity vectorEntity() {
  506:         return new ScientificFeatureVectorEntity(
  507:                 "SFV-001",
  508:                 "ST-001",
  509:                 "ILP_SCIENTIFIC_BASELINE_V1",
  510:                 "SCIENTIFIC_FEATURE_GENERATOR_V1",
  511:                 CUTOFF,
  512:                 COMPLETED_AT,
  513:                 2,
  514:                 ScientificFeatureVectorStatus.COMPLETED,
  515:                 "CHECKSUM-001"
  516:         );
  517:     }
  518: 
  519:     private void assertCommonFields(
  520:             ScientificFeatureGenerationRun actual,
  521:             ScientificFeatureGenerationRun expected
  522:     ) {
  523:         assertThat(actual.id())
  524:                 .isEqualTo(expected.id());
  525: 
  526:         assertThat(actual.participantId())
  527:                 .isEqualTo(
  528:                         expected.participantId()
  529:                 );
  530: 
  531:         assertThat(actual.featureSetVersion())
  532:                 .isEqualTo(
  533:                         expected.featureSetVersion()
  534:                 );
  535: 
  536:         assertThat(actual.generatorVersion())
  537:                 .isEqualTo(
  538:                         expected.generatorVersion()
  539:                 );
  540: 
  541:         assertThat(actual.featureCutoffAt())
  542:                 .isEqualTo(
  543:                         expected.featureCutoffAt()
  544:                 );
  545: 
  546:         assertThat(actual.startedAt())
  547:                 .isEqualTo(expected.startedAt());
  548: 
  549:         assertThat(actual.inputObservationCount())
  550:                 .isEqualTo(
  551:                         expected.inputObservationCount()
  552:                 );
  553:     }

  236:                         "CHECKSUM-CASCADE"
  237:                 );
  238: 
  239:         vector.addItem(

  244:                         "KOLB_V1",
  245:                         "ADMIN-001"
  246:                 )
  247:         );

  250:

  252:                 .isEqualTo(1);
  253:

  255:         vectorRepository.flush();
  256: 
  257:         assertThat(itemRepository.count())
  258:                 .isZero();
  259:     }
